Abstract: We present a deep-learning pipeline for enhancing the detection of faint moving objects in optical space situational awareness (SSA) imagery through automated star removal and background reconstruction. Detecting low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) objects remains extremely challenging in optical observations, particularly in the cislunar (X-GEO) environment, where structured sky backgrounds, dense stellar fields, and scattered moonlight significantly degrade the performance of classical detection algorithms. To address this problem, the proposed pipeline combines a lightweight segmentation network (Tiny-U-Net) to generate stellar masks with a partial-convolution variational autoencoder (astro-VAE), designed to learn the statistical distribution of astronomical backgrounds and perform context-aware inpainting of masked regions. The reconstructed background maps can then be used as a preprocessing step to suppress fixed sources and background inhomogeneities prior to detection. As a proof of concept, the approach is integrated with a shift-and-stack scheme and evaluated on real ground-based telescope observations targeting the X-GEO region. Results demonstrate that the method reconstructs star-free backgrounds with high fidelity, while preserving moving targets and significantly enhancing detectability, thereby providing an effective data-driven preprocessing strategy for faint moving-object detection in optical SSA scenarios.

</details>


### [36] [Order-Aware 2.5D Multiple Instance Learning for Preoperative MRI-Based Perineural Invasion Risk Assessment in Intrahepatic Cholangiocarcinoma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11271)
*Hyunsu Go,Youngung Han,Kyeonghun Kim,Jinyong Jun,Junbeom Lee,Dohyun Kweon,Yului Jeong,Suah Park,Sungha Park,Anna Jung,Woo Kyoung Jeong,Ken Ying-Kai Liao,Hyuk-Jae Lee,Nam-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出弱监督框架OAS-MIL，仅用患者级标签从术前T2加权MRI预测肝内胆管癌的周围神经侵犯（PNI），平均AUROC达0.770，优于体积与MIL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PNI是肝内胆管癌的不良组织病理学标志物，但通常只能在手术切除后确诊。利用术前T2加权MRI进行无创预测有助于术前评估，但存在标注困难：仅有患者级标签，缺少切片级或体素级标注。

Method: 提出Order-Aware Slab Multiple Instance Learning（OAS-MIL）：以肿瘤为中心的MRI裁剪被表示为若干重叠2.5D slab构成的有序序列，slab由相邻轴位切片构成；共享编码器提取slab级特征，再由置换不变集合注意力分支与双向序列注意力分支聚合。

Result: 在患者级五折标签分层交叉验证下，OAS-MIL平均AUROC为0.770，优于所评估的体积（volumetric）与MIL基线方法。

Conclusion: 结果表明轴位顺序为MRI弱监督PNI预测提供了有效的归纳偏置。

Abstract: Perineural invasion (PNI) is an adverse histopathologic marker in intrahepatic cholangiocarcinoma (ICC), but it is usually confirmed only after resection. Preoperative T2-weighted MRI may provide noninvasive imaging cues predictive of PNI, although labels are available only at the patient level without slice- or voxel-level annotations. We propose Order-Aware Slab Multiple Instance Learning (OAS-MIL), a weakly supervised framework for patient-level PNI prediction. Each tumor-centered MRI crop is represented as an ordered sequence of overlapping 2.5D slabs formed from contiguous axial slices. A shared encoder extracts slab-level features, which are aggregated by a permutation-invariant set-attention branch and a bidirectional sequence-attention branch. Using five-fold label-stratified cross-validation at the patient level, OAS-MIL achieved a mean AUROC of 0.770, outperforming the evaluated volumetric and MIL baselines. These results suggest that axial order provides a useful inductive bias for weakly supervised PNI prediction from MRI.

</details>


### [37] [SAMV-DUSt3R: Instance-Centric 3D Scene Decoupling from Sparse Multi-Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11279)
*Langxu Zhao,Zuan Gu,Yingdan Zhang,Pengfei Zhao,Tianhan Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出端到端模型SAMV-DUSt3R，将SAM2的2D掩码注入MV-DUSt3R重建流程，实现物体级解耦重建，提升形状精度与重建稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D场景中物体解耦（将物体从三维场景中分离）的需求日益增长，现有方法可能依赖多阶段流水线，效率和精度受限。

Method: 在MV-DUSt3R重建中注入SAM2的2D掩码；设计Cross Flow Mask Block利用掩码引导网络聚焦目标实例，实现物体级解耦；引入轻量级Spatial RankGNN选择最优参考视角。

Result: Spatial RankGNN参考视角选择准确率达73.5%；相比SOTA基线，各项指标上平均重建精度提升11%；展现出强实例解耦能力。

Conclusion: 该端到端框架无需多阶段流水线即可实现物体级解耦与精度提升，对自动驾驶、机器人、AR/VR及文化遗产数字化等应用有明显价值。

Abstract: With the rising demand to decouple objects from 3D scenes, we propose SAMV-DUSt3R, an end-to-end model that injects SAM2 2D masks into MV-DUSt3R reconstruction. A Cross Flow Mask Block uses these masks to steer the network toward the target instance, jointly improving shape accuracy and achieving object-level disentanglement without multi-stage pipelines. To ensure reconstruction stability, a lightweight Spatial RankGNN selects the optimal reference view with a selection accuracy of 73.5\%. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method boosts average reconstruction precision by 11\% across various metrics compared to state-of-the-art baselines. These results reveal a strong instance-disentanglement capability and clear benefits for driving, robotics, AR/VR, and heritage digitisation.

</details>


### [38] [Your Model Already Knows Don't Teach It, Learn to Ask It: Soft Prompting for Few-Shot Adaptation of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11310)
*Gautam Rajendrakumar Gare,Siyi Li,Hewei Wang,Cesar Daniel Hernandez,Wei Zhao,Wolfgang M. Pauli,John Galeotti,Deva Ramanan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在跨域小样本目标检测中，软提示（soft prompting）仅需约1-3个连续提示token（平均7,168参数）即可在Roboflow20-VL上达到14.2 mAP（10-shot），与最优LoRA配置持平，但训练参数少超过20,000倍，且不会造成预训练能力遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在航空、工业、医疗等跨域场景下，视觉语言模型（VLM）的小样本目标检测仅能用十张标注图像进行监督。现有适配方法（离散提示优化、LoRA微调）各有局限，作者希望重新审视被忽视的第三种方案——软提示，以验证冻结主干、仅优化少量连续提示token是否足够胜任，并探讨其相对LoRA的代价与收益。

Method: 保持预训练主干冻结，仅优化少量连续提示token（软提示）。研究两个关键设计选择：提示token的插入位置（置于视觉与文本token之间的跨模态边界）以及初始化方式（从空空间token初始化优于语义与随机初始化）。后将同一思路扩展到RoboCasa操作任务，在冻结的π0.5视觉-语言-动作策略上，将token放在梯度瓶颈处以进行软提示。

Result: 跨模态边界放置提示token优于其他位置（10.0 vs. 8.4 mAP）；空空间token初始化最佳。1-3个学习token（平均7,168参数）在Roboflow20-VL（10-shot）上达到14.2 mAP，与最佳LoRA配置持平，参数少20,000倍以上。软提示优化难度更高，跨随机种子方差更大；但不造成遗忘：精度相当的LoRA rank使NaturalBench VQA相对下降35%（最大rank达56%），而软提示保持预训练性能不变。学习token表现为提示而非权重，可无重训迁移至新模型（Qwen3.5-9B上+0.8 mAP），并可文本化为可读提示，效果与DetPO持平、优于GEPA。在RoboCasa操作任务上，冻结的π0.5策略在三个任务中的两个上匹配LoRA基线。

Conclusion: 现代VLM已编码了专业领域所需的大部分知识，关键挑战在于“如何提问”。软提示以极少参数实现与LoRA相当的跨域小样本检测性能，且无遗忘、可迁移、可文本化，并具备跨任务与跨模态的通用性，是值得重视的高效适配范式。

Abstract: We address few-shot object detection with vision-language models (VLMs) in out-of-domain settings such as aerial, industrial, and medical imagery, using only ten annotated images for supervision. Existing adaptation methods are discrete prompt optimization and LoRA fine-tuning. We revisit a third option: soft prompting, where a small number of continuous prompt tokens are optimized while the pretrained backbone remains frozen.
  We identify two key design choices. First, placing prompt tokens at the cross-modal boundary between visual and text tokens outperforms other placements (10.0 vs. 8.4 mAP). Second, initializing prompts from the empty space token outperforms semantic and random initialization.
  With these choices, one to three learned tokens (7,168 parameters on average) match the best LoRA configuration on Roboflow20-VL (14.2 mAP, 10-shot) while training over 20,000x fewer parameters. Soft prompting remains harder to optimize, exhibiting higher variance across random seeds. Unlike LoRA, however, it causes no forgetting: the LoRA rank matching our accuracy reduces NaturalBench VQA accuracy by 35% relative, rising to 56% at the largest rank, whereas soft prompting leaves pretrained performance unchanged.
  The learned tokens behave like prompts rather than weights. They transfer to a newer model without retraining (+0.8 mAP on Qwen3.5-9B) and can be verbalized into readable prompts competitive with prompt-search methods (matching DetPO and outperforming GEPA).
  The approach also extends beyond detection. On RoboCasa manipulation tasks, the frozen $π_{0.5}$ vision-language-action policy benefits from soft prompting, matching the LoRA baseline on two of three tasks when tokens are placed at the gradient bottleneck. These results suggest modern VLMs already encode much of what is needed for specialized domains; the challenge is learning how to ask.

</details>


### [39] [MC-DeTra: Motion-Consistent Joint Object Detection and Socially-Aware Trajectory Forecasting in Bird's-Eye-View Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11717)
*Vladislav Diuzhev,Dmitry Yudin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文公开复现了DeTra，并提出MC-DeTra：一组运动一致性机制，通过两个由标注导出的辅助信号（历史运动、周围交通占用）及一个输出间一致性约束，在训练阶段塑造共享BEV表示，推理阶段移除，不增加推理延迟，在Waymo数据集上改善了动态目标的轨迹预测且不损失检测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一检测与轨迹预测模型在动态运动目标上的精度仍是最难的部分，且最强的DeTra模型没有公开实现，阻碍了复现与进一步研究。

Method: 在公开复现的DeTra基础上提出MC-DeTra：引入基于标注的两个辅助监督信号——每个actor观察到的历史运动、以及构成其社会情境的周围交通占用——外加一个将预测朝向与预测运动方向对齐的输出间一致性约束；所有损失仅用于训练，测试时移除。

Result: 在Waymo Open Dataset上，采用严格的检测条件化预测协议评估，MC-DeTra改善了动态且具有社会情境的轨迹预测，同时保持或提升检测精度；基于梯度的损失校准分析揭示了辅助目标在共享骨干网络处的竞争；消融实验识别了贡献最大的信号。

Conclusion: MC-DeTra通过训练期运动一致性监督有效提升统一检测-预测模型对动态目标的预测能力，且不增加推理开销；作者公开了代码、配置与评估工具以促进复现研究。

Abstract: Unified models for object detection and trajectory forecasting aim to merge perception and prediction for autonomous driving, refining actor trajectories directly over shared bird's-eye-view (BEV) images rasterized from LiDAR and high-definition maps. Their accuracy on dynamic, moving actors, however, remains the hardest part of the task, and the strongest such model, DeTra, has no public implementation. We contribute an openly released DeTra reimplementation with documented approximations, and on top of it MC-DeTra: a family of motion-consistency mechanisms that add supervision through two annotation-derived auxiliary signals -- each actor's observed past motion and the occupancy of the surrounding traffic that forms its social context -- and one inter-output consistency constraint that aligns an actor's predicted heading with its predicted direction of motion. Every proposed loss is train-only and inference-safe: it shapes the shared BEV representation during training and is removed at test time, adding no inference latency. On the Waymo Open Dataset, evaluated under a strict, detection-conditioned forecasting protocol, MC-DeTra improves dynamic, socially-situated trajectory forecasting while preserving or improving detection accuracy; a gradient-based loss-calibration analysis exposes how the auxiliary objectives compete at the shared backbone, and our ablation identifies which signals contribute most. We release code, configurations, and evaluation tooling at https://github.com/diuzhevVlad/MC-DeTra.

</details>


### [40] [GRIPNet: Gaussian Radial Intensity Prior Guided Architecture for Pulmonary Nodule Detection in CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11312)
*Haojie Yang,Ran Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现肺结节强度呈中心峰值、径向高斯衰减的规律，据此提出符合该先验的GRIPNet检测器，在三个公开数据集上取得领先mAP与高IoU定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌致死率居恶性肿瘤首位，低剂量CT筛查是早期诊断主要途径，而小于6mm的结节难以检出。现有方法将结节视为通用目标，忽略了其成像物理特性，导致小尺寸结节检测性能受限。

Method: 先通过强度统计验证结节外观的径向高斯规律：在三个公开基准的18,218个标注病灶上拟合，径向决定系数均值在各数据集与尺寸分层中均高于0.86，并指出方形卷积与径向信号不匹配。据此提出GRIPNet：以风车卷积分解径向梯度，双频模块分离边界细节与结构上下文，空洞掩码注意力匹配衰减范围，自适应损失按显著度重加权样本。

Result: GRIPNet在KanserSet、LUNA16、Lung-PET-CT-Dx上mAP@0.5分别达95.3%、91.6%和97.9%，并在实时速度下提升了高IoU定位精度。

Conclusion: 结节强度分布的径向高斯规律是可测量且高度规则的结构先验，将其显式嵌入检测器的各模块可显著提升小尺寸结节检测与定位性能。

Abstract: Lung cancer causes more deaths than any other malignancy, and low-dose CT screening is the main pathway to early diagnosis. That pathway hinges on the smallest lesions, yet nodules below six millimeters remain hard to detect, because most methods treat a nodule as a generic object and ignore the imaging physics behind its appearance. We show that this appearance is highly regular. Intensity peaks at the geometric center of a nodule and decays radially in a Gaussian pattern, and a fit to 18,218 annotated lesions from three public benchmarks yields a mean radial coefficient of determination above 0.86 in every dataset and size stratum. A square convolution samples both axes uniformly and is mismatched to this radial signal, most severely for small nodules. Guided by this evidence, we propose GRIPNet (Gaussian Radial Intensity Prior Network), a detector in which every module maps to a measurable property of the intensity distribution. Pinwheel convolutions decompose radial gradients, a dual-frequency module separates boundary detail from structural context, dilated masked attention matches the decay extent, and an adaptive loss reweights samples by conspicuity. GRIPNet raises mAP@0.5 to 95.3, 91.6 and 97.9 percent on KanserSet, LUNA16 and Lung-PET-CT-Dx while sharpening high-IoU localization at real-time speed.

</details>


### [41] [Mi-Ripple: Restoring Images Degraded by Iterative AI Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11317)
*Jiayin Chen,Yicheng Xu,Muting Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Mi-Ripple是一种诊断引导的修复流程，用于抑制迭代参考条件图像编辑产生的网格状与颗粒状"数字波纹"纹理，同时保护图像结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代参考条件图像编辑会引入网格状和颗粒状纹理（数字波纹），现有方法缺乏对这类伪影的诊断与针对性抑制手段。

Method: 将周期性晶格伪影与内容纠缠的颗粒纹理分离，结合选择性光谱陷波、结构感知平滑以及清洁参考再生三种手段；在伪影可光谱隔离时进行低失真滤波，在滤波会破坏合法细节时改用视觉重建。

Result: 14次纯陷波操作中，全图残差标准差为0.08–0.44（CIELAB亮度单位）；在配对再生示例中，参考清洁使输出碎片密度降低45%。

Conclusion: Mi-Ripple将可测量的伪影减少与视觉上更干净的生成图像联系起来，而非仅优化单一光谱指标。

Abstract: Iterative reference-conditioned image editing can introduce grid-like and granular textures, commonly described as digital ripple. We present Mi-Ripple, a diagnosis-guided restoration workflow that suppresses this digital ripple while protecting image structure. Mi-Ripple separates periodic lattice artifacts from content-entangled granular texture, then combines selective spectral notching, structure-aware smoothing, and cleaned-reference regeneration. This separation enables low-distortion filtering when artifacts are spectrally isolated and visual reconstruction when filtering would erase legitimate detail. Across fourteen notch-only executions, whole-image residual standard deviation is 0.08--0.44 in CIELAB lightness units. In a paired regeneration example, reference cleaning reduces output debris density by 45\%. Mi-Ripple links measurable artifact reduction to visibly cleaner generated images, rather than optimizing a spectral score alone.

</details>


### [42] [Predictive Multi-Landmark OCT Tracking for Increased Motion Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11330)
*Konrad Reuter,Suresh Guttikonda,Chaitali Uday Karekar,Christian Betz,Alexander Schlaefer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于全局位姿预测的OCT预测式追踪方法，通过在多标志点间传播位置更新实现全局位姿预测，在高达100 mm/s速度和9个连续标志点下RMSE低于1 mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCT具有高空间分辨率和固有深度感知能力，适合无标记运动追踪，但现有OCT追踪方法可追踪速度受限，尤其在为6D位姿估计而顺序追踪多个标志点时表现不佳。

Method: 提出预测式追踪方法，在多个被追踪标志点之间传播位置更新，从而获得全局位姿预测，以提升高速运动下的追踪鲁棒性。

Result: 在最高100 mm/s的速度和最多9个连续追踪标志点条件下，RMSE低于1 mm。

Conclusion: 全局运动传播与预测能够有效提升OCT追踪的鲁棒性，为高速无标记6D位姿追踪提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Optical coherence tomography is a promising modality for markerless motion tracking due to its high spatial resolution and inherent depth perception. However, existing OCT-based tracking approaches are limited in terms of trackable velocity, particularly when multiple landmarks are tracked sequentially for 6D pose estimation. In this work, we present a predictive tracking approach that propagates positional updates between multiple tracked landmarks to obtain a global pose prediction. This enables more robust tracking under high velocities. Our results demonstrate RMSEs below 1 mm for velocities up to 100 mm/s and up to nine consecutively tracked landmarks, highlighting the potential of global motion propagation and prediction for improving the robustness of OCT-based tracking.

</details>


### [43] [R4Tun: LLM-guided adaptive segmental tunnel lining segmentation in point clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11360)
*Xinghui Tao,Zehao Ye,Guangming Wang,Jelena Ninić,Brian Sheil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出R4Tun，一个由大语言模型（LLM）驱动的管片隧道衬砌点云自适应分割框架，在SAM4Tun专家流水线上引入基于记忆（m）、状态（s）和知识（k）的受限参数调优，在30个Seg2Tunnel子集上显著提升了mIoU与OA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化管片隧道衬砌检测需要从3D点云中进行自适应分割，但专家调参的流水线在隧道条件变化时性能下降，缺乏可控制的跨条件自适应机制。

Method: R4Tun以专家设计的SAM4Tun流水线为基础，利用LLM进行有界参数调优，调优由结构化上下文驱动，包括记忆（m）、状态（s）与知识（k）三种设置；在30个Seg2Tunnel子集（13个规则、17个复杂）上，使用三种LLM开展30×3×3共270次实验。

Result: 完整m+s+k设计将mIoU从0.18提升至0.43–0.48，OA从0.42提升至0.59–0.65；接近参考的规则（错缝）子集mIoU达0.784–0.796。三种LLM呈现相似的参数调整趋势（均值增益的95%置信区间重叠），并持续调整同一组关键参数。

Conclusion: R4Tun在测试的SAM4Tun–Seg2Tunnel设定下是一种可控、无标签、跨LLM的自适应机制，能够稳定提升精度；论文将其定位为机制性贡献而非可部署的最终检测系统，每次有界参数变更均可通过日志理由进行审计。

Abstract: Automated inspection of segmental tunnel linings requires adaptive segmentation from 3D point clouds, yet expert-tuned pipelines often degrade when tunnel conditions vary. This paper presents R4Tun, a large language model (LLM)-driven adaptation framework that extends an expert-designed pipeline (SAM4Tun) with bounded parameter tuning informed by structured context: memory ($m$), state ($s$), and knowledge ($k$). Evaluated on 30 selected Seg2Tunnel subsets (13 regular, 17 complex) across three LLMs, the full $m+s+k$ design raised mean Intersection-over-Union (mIoU) from 0.18 to 0.43--0.48 and overall accuracy (OA) from 0.42 to 0.59--0.65 relative to the static SAM4Tun baseline, with the near-reference regular (staggered) subsets reaching mIoU 0.784--0.796 across LLMs. Across 270 (30 tunnels $\times$ 3 different LLMs $\times$ 3 context settings) runs, the LLMs showed similar parameter-adjustment trends (with overlapping 95\% CIs on mean gains) and consistently adjusted a shared set of critical parameters. These results support R4Tun as a controlled, label-free, cross-LLM adaptation mechanism in the tested SAM4Tun--Seg2Tunnel setting, demonstrating consistent accuracy gains; we position R4Tun as a mechanism contribution rather than a deployable final-inspection system, in which each bounded parameter change is auditable via logged rationales.

</details>


### [44] [Vision Transformer-Based Multi-Level Feature Fusion for Multi-Label Sewer Defect Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11375)
*Xu Fang,Zhuoran Wang,Qing Li,Shengyu Zhang,Guanzhi Deng,Jianbiao He,Qingquan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出层级视觉Transformer多级特征融合模型Sewer-Transformer-ML及两个轻量模型，兼顾精度与算力，在Sewer-ML榜单登顶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排水管道缺陷自动分类对基础设施状态评估与养护决策至关重要，但现有深度学习方法在大规模多标签场景下难以兼顾分类精度与计算复杂度，且资源受限巡检场景缺乏轻量化方案。

Method: 构建带多级特征融合的层级视觉Transformer模型Sewer-Transformer-ML，并设计两种轻量架构Sewer-MobileNet-ML与Sewer-Mobile-TransNet；开展消融实验对比直接拼接与注意力融合两种特征融合策略在Transformer与多尺度CNN特征上的效果，并测试小样本（1177张）下Sewer-ML预训练的迁移增益。

Result: Sewer-ML测试集上，Sewer-Transformer-ML-Base取得F2_CIW 65.68%、F1_Normal 92.68%，位列公开榜单第一，F2_CIW领先第二名7.6个百分点；Sewer-MobileNet-ML以仅17M参数达到F2_CIW 65.73%，较基础模型参数减少约95%；在Sewer-Capsule标准划分下，Sewer-Mobile-TransNet分类准确率达96.43%；训练集缩减至1177张时，Sewer-ML预训练稳定提升性能；消融表明Transformer特征宜直接拼接，多尺度CNN特征宜用注意力融合。

Conclusion: 研究为排水管道自动化巡检、轻量化模型设计及跨土木基础设施检测平台的适配提供了算力与性能依据。

Abstract: Automated classification of sewer defects is essential for infrastructure condition assessment and maintenance decision-making, but existing deep learning methods struggle to balance classification accuracy and computational complexity in large-scale multi-label scenarios. This study develops Sewer-Transformer-ML, a hierarchical vision Transformer with multi-level feature fusion, together with two lightweight architectures, Sewer-MobileNet-ML and Sewer-Mobile-TransNet, for resource-constrained inspection scenarios. On the Sewer-ML test set, Sewer-Transformer-ML-Base achieved an $F2_{\text{CIW}}$ of 65.68% and an $F1_{\text{Normal}}$ of 92.68%, ranking first on the public leaderboard and exceeding the second-ranked method by 7.6 percentage points in $F2_{\text{CIW}}$. Sewer-MobileNet-ML achieved an $F2_{\text{CIW}}$ of 65.73% with only 17 M parameters, representing an approximately 95% parameter reduction relative to the base model. Under the standard Sewer-Capsule data split, Sewer-Mobile-TransNet achieved 96.43% classification accuracy. When the training set was reduced to 1,177 images, pretraining on Sewer-ML consistently improved model performance. Ablation experiments further showed that direct concatenation was more effective for Transformer features, whereas attention-based fusion better supported multiscale CNN features. These findings provide a computational basis for automated sewer inspection, lightweight model design, and adaptation across civil infrastructure inspection platforms.

</details>


### [45] [Brain-PACE: A Deep Siamese MRI Framework for Modelling Longitudinal Brain Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11378)
*Samuel Maddox,Jacob Newman,Saber Sami,Michal Mackiewicz,for the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative,the Australian Imaging Biomarkers,Lifestyle flagship study of ageing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Brain-PACE：基于配对T1 MRI的纵向脑龄加速模型，直接估计脑结构老化速率，拓展LILAC框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑龄估计虽用于评估脑健康与疾病，但脑老化的纵向轨迹定义不清，临床可用性有限；且已有方法多为重复横断面估计间接推算老化速度，可能遗漏纵向信息。

Method: 基于Siamese纵向框架开发Brain-PACE，直接从配对T1加权MRI估计结构脑老化速率；方法上扩展LILAC框架，结合空间注意力、软标签分布学习与Cramér距离目标函数，提升概率性能、降低预测偏差并给出预测不确定性。

Result: 在轻度认知障碍参与者中42.6%检出加速老化；更快的Brain-PACE与更严重的功能与认知损害相关（FAQ r=0.35；ADAS13 r=0.30；CDR-SB r=0.32），并与后扣带回（r=0.59）、楔前叶（r=0.47）、内嗅皮层（r=0.37）区域tau负荷相关；上述关联强于由重复横断面脑龄估计间接计算的速率。

Conclusion: Brain-PACE是一种补充性的纵向影像表型，对早期神经变性中的临床与生物学变化具有敏感性，直接纵向建模可捕获与持续病理改变相关的互补信息。

Abstract: Brain age estimation has become a popular research proxy for assessing brain health and disease, yet longitudinal trajectories of brain ageing are still poorly defined, and clinical use is limited. Building on existing Siamese longitudinal frameworks, we develop Brain-Predicted Age Acceleration (Brain-PACE) to directly estimate the pace of structural brain ageing from paired T1-weighted MRI. Brain-PACE identified accelerated ageing in $42.6$% of participants with mild cognitive impairment. Faster Brain-PACE was associated with greater functional and cognitive impairment (FAQ; $r=0.35$, ADAS13; $r=0.30$, CDR-SB; $r=0.32$) and greater regional tau burden in the posterior cingulate ($r=0.59$), precuneus ($r=0.47$), and entorhinal cortex ($r=0.37$). These associations were stronger than those observed when pace was calculated indirectly from repeated cross-sectional brain age estimates, suggesting that direct longitudinal modelling captures complementary information relevant to ongoing pathological change. Methodologically, Brain-PACE extends the LILAC framework by combining spatial attention with soft label distribution learning and a Cramér distance objective, improving probabilistic performance and reducing prediction bias while providing measures of predictive uncertainty. Together, these findings support Brain-PACE as a complementary longitudinal imaging phenotype with sensitivity to relevant clinical and biological changes in early neurodegeneration.

</details>


### [46] [DINO-Med: A Unified Patch-Based Adaptation Framework for Multi-Modal Medical Image Analysis Applied to Liver Fibrosis Staging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11380)
*Boya Wang,Ruizhe Li,Chao Chen,Xin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一的基于patch的多模态医学影像框架，将自然图像基础模型DINOv3适配到医学领域，以肝纤维化分期为案例，DINOv3冻结特征在S1/S4分类上显著优于Radiomics、ResNet、SAM-Med2D。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然彩色图像与多通道医学扫描之间存在显著域差异，将DINOv3等自然图像基础模型直接迁移到多模态医学影像面临困难，需要探索有效的适配与特征利用策略。

Method: 构建统一的patch-based框架：免训练配准、自动定位、掩膜过滤的patch提取，并以分层策略聚合patch级结果得到受试者级诊断。以肝纤维化分期为案例，对比四类patch级特征表示（手工Radiomics、ResNet、预训练SAM-Med2D、冻结DINOv3），所有模型共享同一轻量MLP头，并在刚性/可变形两种配准下评估。训练仅用轻度纤维化S1与肝硬化S4两类。

Result: 在CARE 2025 Liver Track 4队列360名受试者上，经10次随机90%/10%训练/测试划分评估，DINOv3框架显著优于所有基线，S1最佳准确率78.4%，S4为75.8%。

Conclusion: 冻结的DINOv3特征经统一patch框架适配后，可有效用于多模态医学影像，单一分类器即可兼顾显著纤维化检测与肝硬化分期。

Abstract: Adapting natural-image foundation models like DINOv3 to multi-modal medical imaging is challenging due to the significant domain gap between natural color images and multi-channel medical scans. We present a unified, patch-based framework that processes raw multimodal imaging through training-free registration, automated localization, and mask-filtered patch extraction. This architecture culminates in a hierarchical strategy that aggregates patch-level insights into subject-level diagnostics. Using liver fibrosis staging as a case study, we evaluate four patch-level feature representations: handcrafted Radiomics features, learned ResNet features, pre-trained foundation model SAM-Med2D features, and frozen DINOv3 features. To ensure a controlled comparison, all models utilize the same lightweight MLP head and are evaluated across both rigid and deformable registration settings. Our training protocol focuses on mild fibrosis (S1) and cirrhosis (S4) classes only, enabling a single classifier to address both substantial fibrosis detection and cirrhosis staging. Evaluated via 10 random train (90%)/ test (10%) splits on 360 subjects from the CARE 2025 Liver Track 4 cohort, our DINOv3-based framework significantly outperforms all baselines, achieving the best classification accuracy of 78.4% for S1 and 75.8% for S4.

</details>


### [47] [Multi-Modal Controlled Coherent Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11439)
*Yifei Liu,Qiong Cao,Hongwei Yi,Huaiguang Jiang,Changxing Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MOCO——一种基于扩散模型的框架，可在无需对齐多模态数据的条件下，同时处理语音、文本与轨迹输入，生成协调自然的3D数字人全身动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类可自然地边走边说，但现有3D动作生成方法受限于对齐多模态数据的稀缺，通常按模态逐个或加权求和组合动作，导致动作不匹配、不真实。因此需要一种能处理同步多模态输入、且不依赖对齐数据的动作生成方法。

Method: MOCO采用扩散框架，关键创新在于解耦动作生成过程：在每个去噪步骤中，扩散模型从输入噪声出发，针对每个模态独立生成动作，并按预设空间规则组装身体部位；组合后的动作再被加噪，作为下一步去噪的输入噪声。如此迭代，使各模态在整体动作语境中逐步修正自身贡献，实现跨模态的渐进协调与同步。

Result: 在专门构建的多模态基准上进行评估，实验结果表明MOCO优于现有基线方法，推动了3D数字人多模态动作生成领域的发展。

Conclusion: 通过解耦式迭代扩散与空间规则组装，MOCO在不依赖对齐多模态数据的前提下实现了自然、流畅且同步的多模态动作生成，验证了该范式的有效性。

Abstract: It is natural for humans to walk and talk simultaneously. This paper tackles the challenge of replicating such natural behaviors in 3D avatar motion generation driven by concurrent multimodal inputs, such as a text description of a man walking alongside speech audio. Existing methods, constrained by the scarcity of aligned multimodal data, typically combine motions from individual modalities sequentially or through weighted sums. However, they often result in mismatched or unrealistic movements. To overcome these limitations, we propose MOCO, a novel diffusion-based framework capable of processing multiple simultaneous inputs, including speech audio, text descriptions, and trajectory data, to generate coherent and lifelike motions without requiring aligned multimodal data. Our key innovation lies in decoupling the motion generation process. During each denoising step, the diffusion model independently generates motions for each modality from the input noise and assembles the body parts according to predefined spatial rules. The resulting combined motion is then diffused and serves as the input noise for the subsequent denoising step. This iterative approach enables each modality to refine its contribution within the context of the overall motion, progressively harmonizing movements across modalities. Consequently, the generated motions become increasingly natural and fluid with each iteration, achieving coherent and synchronized behaviors. We evaluate our approach using a purpose-built multimodal benchmark. Experimental results demonstrate that MOCO outperforms existing baselines, advancing the field of multimodal motion generation for 3D avatars.

</details>


### [48] [BruNet: A Cross-Domain Transfer Framework for Bruise Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11463)
*Qiming Wang,Richard J. Motley,Ebube E. Obi,Xianfang Sun,Paul L. Rosin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 BruNet，一个将 ViT 视觉编码器（自监督 DINOv3 或预训练 LingBot-Vision 骨干）与 SAM 掩码解码器结合的分割框架，用于淤伤（bruise）的像素级分割。模型在 HAM10000 皮肤病变数据集上训练，并在未额外微调的情况下在独立淤伤数据集上评估。据作者所述，这是首个针对自动淤伤分割的研究，BruNet 优于 CNN 模型、SOTA 分割模型、ChatGPT-4o/5 辅助的 SAM2 零样本基线以及 MedSAM，展现出强跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像中淤伤分割存在数据与标注有限、边界弥散、外观高度多变等挑战。已有研究多聚焦于淤伤的检测、分类或颜色分析，而非像素级定位，因此该任务尚属空白，作者将其视为首个自动淤伤分割研究。

Method: 提出 BruNet 框架：视觉编码器采用 ViT 结构（自监督 DINOv3 或预训练 LingBot-Vision 骨干），搭配基于 SAM 的掩码解码器。训练使用 HAM10000 皮肤病变数据集；评估在独立的淤伤数据集上进行，且不进行额外微调（零样本跨域设置）。对比对象包括 CNN 模型、SOTA 分割模型、ChatGPT-4o/5 辅助的 SAM2 零样本基线与医学领域 MedSAM。

Result: BruNet 在淤伤分割上的表现优于 CNN 模型、当前最先进的分割模型、ChatGPT-4o/5 辅助的 SAM2 零样本基线以及医学导向的 MedSAM，体现出较强的跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: BruNet 通过 ViT 编码器与 SAM 解码器的组合，在未针对淤伤数据微调的条件下实现了优于多种基线的分割性能，首次系统性地解决了自动淤伤分割问题，并验证了跨域泛化潜力。

Abstract: Segmenting bruises is a challenging task in medical imaging due to limited data and annotations, diffuse boundaries, and highly variable appearance. In this work, we propose BruNet, a segmentation framework that combines a ViT-based visual encoder (a self-supervised DINOv3 or a pretrained LingBot-Vision backbone) with a SAM-based mask decoder. BruNet is trained on the HAM10000 skin lesion dataset and evaluated on a separate bruise dataset without additional fine-tuning. Although a small number of prior studies have explored machine learning and computer vision for bruise analysis, existing work has primarily focused on detection, classification, or colour analysis rather than pixel-level localisation. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study to address automatic bruise segmentation. Our results show that BruNet outperforms CNN-based models, state-of-the-art segmentation models, ChatGPT-4o/5-assisted SAM2 zero-shot baselines, and the medical-oriented MedSAM model, demonstrating strong cross-domain generalisation to bruise segmentation.

</details>


### [49] [BridgeMatch: Conditional Transport Bridges in Matching Matrix Space for 3D Deformable Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11472)
*Qianliang Wu,Haobo Jiang,Guangwei Gao,Shuo Chen,Jin Xie,Jian Yang,Yaqing Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 \paper，一种两阶段生成式求解器，在粗、细两个分辨率上均保持完整的软匹配矩阵，用于可靠的非刚性点云对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非刚性点云对应在可变形解剖配准、具身感知与操作、动态三维重建中至关重要。常见的由粗到细方法通过选取 top-K 粗区域来降低计算量，但这种剪枝会剔除一些弱却正确的假设，使精细匹配被限制在不完整的搜索空间内，从而损害对应可靠性，尤其在低重叠场景下。

Method: 方法为两阶段生成式框架。阶段一在紧致的粗分辨率空间中使用去噪扩散估计全局匹配矩阵；随后将该矩阵提升到高分辨率并保持其层级结构，所得提升矩阵具有秩有界且块常数的性质。阶段二通过条件传输桥对其进行精化，桥梁以两类动力学实现：确定性的端点参数化条件流匹配（CFM）ODE，以及受薛定谔桥启发的随机布朗桥 SDE。两种变体共享提升后的源分布、时间条件 Transformer 以及匹配矩阵端点预测器。

Result: 在 4DMatch 与 4DLoMatch 上的实验表明，两种变体均比对比方法产生更准确的对应，并改善下游配准，在低重叠情形下增益更大。在 CAPE 与 DeepDeform 上，无需目标域自适应、使用同一变形求解器即可提升跨数据集泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过在整个粗到细过程中保留完整软匹配矩阵而非剪枝，并借助扩散初始化与条件传输桥精化，可在不牺牲完整搜索空间的前提下兼顾效率与精度，提升低重叠对应与跨数据集泛化。

Abstract: Reliable non-rigid point cloud correspondences are important for deformable anatomical registration, embodied perception and manipulation, and dynamic 3D reconstruction. Coarse-to-fine methods reduce computational cost by selecting the top-\(K\) coarse regions. However, this pruning may remove weak but correct hypotheses and restrict fine matching to an incomplete search space. We present \paper, a two-stage generative solver that maintains the complete soft matching matrix at both coarse and high resolutions. Stage~I uses denoising diffusion to estimate a global matching matrix in the compact coarse-resolution space. We then lift this matrix to high resolution while preserving its hierarchy. The lifted matrix is rank-bounded and block-constant. Stage~II refines it through a conditional transport bridge. We implement the bridge with two types of dynamics: a deterministic endpoint-parameterized conditional Flow Matching (CFM) ODE and a stochastic Brownian-bridge SDE inspired by Schrödinger bridges. Both variants share the lifted source, a time-conditioned transformer, and a matching-matrix endpoint predictor. Experiments on 4DMatch and 4DLoMatch show that both variants produce more accurate correspondences than the compared methods and improve downstream registration, with larger gains in low-overlap cases. They also improve cross-dataset generalization on CAPE and DeepDeform without target-domain adaptation while using the same deformation solver.

</details>


### [50] [Pre- and Post-Treatment Brain Metastases Segmentation Using nnU-Net with Post-Processing for BraTS 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11477)
*Haobin Liu,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文基于5折nnU-Net ResEnc-L集成与规则后处理流程，面向脑转移瘤分割任务，在官方验证集达到LW-DSC 0.733/0.751/0.713/0.549（ET/TC/WT/RC），并通过OOF分析审计各后处理阶段的稳健性，另附LW-DSC机理分析与13项负面结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑转移瘤在病灶大小、强化模式及治疗后表现上存在高度病灶间异质性，导致治疗前与治疗后病例的体积分割成为BraTS 2026 Task 1的核心挑战；同时LW-DSC这一检测导向指标与传统全局Dice行为差异显著，现有方法缺乏对其的机理理解与可靠的稳健性验证。

Method: 构建实用型流水线：5折nnU-Net ResEnc-L集成，每折在1,296例四模态训练数据上独立训练1,000轮，采用标准Dice+交叉熵损失；随后接规则式后处理级联，针对病灶级Dice（LW-DSC）调优；并用无模型训练泄漏的五折OOF分析，以官方BraTS_evaluation代码对每个后处理阶段逐一审计。

Result: 官方验证集LW-DSC为ET 0.733、TC 0.751、WT 0.713、RC 0.549；OOF审计确认两个后处理阶段为稳健且折间一致的提升，第三个阶段仅提升排行榜成绩而无法在OOF中复现；同时给出LW-DSC的机理分析，解释召回恢复型后处理风险低而连通域删除风险高的原因，并报告覆盖损失函数设计、替代骨干网络与推理期设置的13项负面结果。

Conclusion: 该工作表明排行榜增益需经OOF审计验证，规则式后处理应以稳健性和折间一致性为准而非仅看榜单；对LW-DSC的机理认识可指导后处理设计，避免高风险的连通域删除操作；13项负面结果对常见直觉提出反例，源代码以Apache-2.0开源。

Abstract: Brain metastases exhibit high inter-lesion variability in size, enhancement pattern, and post-treatment appearance, making volumetric segmentation of both pre- and post-treatment cases the central challenge of the BraTS 2026 Task 1 (Brain Metastases). We build a pragmatic pipeline on a 5-fold nnU-Net ResEnc-L ensemble, in which each fold is trained independently for 1,000 epochs with the standard Dice + cross-entropy loss on 1,296 four-modality training cases. This ensemble is followed by a rule-based post-processing cascade tuned for the lesion-wise Dice similarity coefficient (LW-DSC), a detection-oriented metric that behaves very differently from the traditional global Dice. The final pipeline reaches an LW-DSC of 0.733 / 0.751 / 0.713 / 0.549 on the enhancing tumour (ET), tumour core (TC), whole tumour (WT), and resection cavity (RC) sub-regions on the official validation leaderboard. Rather than trusting these leaderboard gains, we audit every post-processing stage with a five-fold out-of-fold (OOF) analysis with no model-training leakage over all 1,296 training cases, scored with the official BraTS evaluation code (BraTS_evaluation): it confirms two stages as robust, per-fold-consistent improvements while the third improves only the leaderboard and does not reproduce out-of-fold. We further provide a mechanistic analysis of the LW-DSC metric that explains why recall-recovering post-processing carries low risk whereas component deletion does not, and we report thirteen negative results spanning loss engineering, alternative backbones, and inference-time settings, several of which run counter to widely held intuitions. Source code is released under Apache-2.0 at https://github.com/hornbeamliu/brats2026-met.

</details>


### [51] [FreeFlow: A Bias-free Hierarchical Transformer for Optical Flow Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11486)
*Vladislav Bargatin,Alexander Yakovenko,Khaled Abud,Dmitriy Vatolin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeFlow 是一种不依赖光流专用归纳偏置的层级式 Transformer 光流方法，仅用单一前馈编码器-解码器，通过组合窗口、移位窗口与低分辨率全局注意力实现 SOTA 精度与内存效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光流方法普遍依赖任务特定归纳偏置（如相关体积、特征 warping、迭代细化），这些偏置将模型限制在预设启发式范围内，削弱了表达能力，并带来更复杂的流程与额外计算开销。作者希望验证：在不引入光流专用组件的前提下，能否达到甚至超越现有精度。

Method: 提出 FreeFlow，一种层级式 Transformer，完全不含光流专用组件，采用单一前馈编码器-解码器结构。其核心是三种注意力变体的组合：窗口注意力负责局部处理，移位窗口注意力实现跨窗口信息交互，以及在降采样分辨率上运行的全局注意力。该架构可随模型容量自然扩展。

Result: FreeFlow 从轻量到大型变体均获得一致精度提升。在主要基准上取得最先进结果：Sintel 上 Clean/Final EPE 为 0.68/1.48，KITTI-2015 上 Fl-all 为 3.23，Spring 上 1px 为 3.192；同时在 1080p 推理下保持内存高效。

Conclusion: 实验表明，即便缺乏标准光流归纳偏置，纯粹的层级式 Transformer 编码器-解码器也能在光流任务上达到 SOTA，并具备良好的可扩展性与内存效率，为简化光流流程、减少任务特定设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Optical flow methods typically rely on task-specific inductive biases, such as correlation volumes, feature warping, and iterative refinement, among others, to reach high accuracy. While effective, such biases constrain the model to predefined heuristics, which can limit its expressivity and lead to more complex pipelines and additional computational cost. We present FreeFlow, a hierarchical transformer built without any flow-specific components, using instead a single feed-forward encoder--decoder. FreeFlow combines three attention variants: window attention for local processing, shifted-window attention for cross-window information exchange, and a global attention operating at a reduced resolution. The resulting architecture scales naturally with model capacity, enabling a consistent accuracy gain from small to large variants. Despite the absence of standard inductive biases, FreeFlow achieves state-of-the-art results on major benchmarks, including Sintel (0.68/1.48 EPE on Clean/Final), KITTI-2015 (3.23 Fl-all), and Spring (3.192 1px), while remaining memory efficient at 1080p inference.

</details>


### [52] [Recursive Code World Models: Building Complex Worlds through Recursive Scene Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11499)
*Zhiqi Li,Yuxuan Liao,Bo Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RCWM 提出递归代码世界模型框架，能从单张参考图像以递归方式重建复杂3D世界为可执行代码，性能优于已有代码式图像到场景重建方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码世界模型将世界表示为可执行程序，但仅有表示形式不足以确定如何构建复杂世界，因此需要一套从视觉证据出发、可扩展的构建机制。

Method: RCWM 将递归场景程序（RSP）表示与递归自调用的构建求解器耦合。RSP 以组合式场景代码表示可执行世界；每次求解调用均遵循“建立整体—递归重建未解析部分—重访整体以精修组合”的完整流程，形成全局—局部—全局递归。参考对齐视图在各层级传播共享相机投影，父级重访处理局部精修后出现的边界、空间关系与共享误差；由视觉语言编码智能体直接比较参考图像与场景渲染，指导精修、递归下探与返回。

Result: 在复杂场景上，RCWM 优于先前的基于代码的图像到场景重建方法；消融实验支持递归构建的收益，并表明更深的递归调用可改善更细尺度的重建。

Conclusion: RCWM 为从视觉证据构建复杂可执行世界提供了一种递归构建原则。

Abstract: Code world models represent worlds as executable programs, but this representation alone does not determine how to construct a complex world. We introduce Recursive Code World Models (RCWM), a framework for reconstructing complex 3D worlds in code from a single reference image. RCWM couples a Recursive Scene Program (RSP) representation with a construction solver that recursively calls itself. An RSP represents the executable world as compositional scene code, while each solver call follows the same complete process: establish the whole, recursively reconstruct unresolved parts, and revisit the whole to refine their composition. This global-local-global recursion gives fine-scale structures their own perception-and-editing loops while preserving scene-wide geometry and relationships. Reference-aligned views propagate a shared camera projection across levels, while parent revisitation addresses boundaries, spatial relations, and shared errors that emerge after local refinement. A vision-language coding agent directly compares reference images with scene renders to guide refinement, recursive descent, and return. Across complex scenes, RCWM outperforms prior code-based image-to-scene reconstruction methods. Ablation studies further support the benefits of recursive construction and suggest that deeper calls can improve finer-scale reconstruction. RCWM provides a recursive construction principle for building complex executable worlds from visual evidence.

</details>


### [53] [UBone3D: Physics-Rectified Conditional Flow Matching for Anatomical 3D Shape Completion from Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11506)
*Weiying Chen,Yuchong Gao,Siyuan Li,Marek Reformat,Rui Zheng,Edmond Lou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 UBone3D 框架，基于物理校正的条件流匹配（CFM），直接从带有严重伪影的三维超声部分点云中完成解剖结构重建，在仿真与在体数据上均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维超声是一种安全、无辐射的补充模态，适合与 CT/X 射线配合作纵向监测，但其分割得到的是伪影严重的部分点云，从中恢复干净、完整的解剖结构十分困难。

Method: UBone3D 采用物理校正的条件流匹配（CFM）框架：通过仿真物理代理建模确定性伪影（表面增厚、条纹、数据缺失等），并引入测试时物理校正引导形状补全。推理时补全由两股解耦力共同驱动：(1) 由 CT 训练的生成式形状先验 BoneFM 保证解剖合理性；(2) 由 USimNet 在超声形成空间中保证物理一致性。

Result: 在仿真数据和在体数据上的大量实验表明，该方法在重建精度和解剖保真度上相较现有基线均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 将物理伪影建模与生成式形状先验相结合的条件流匹配方案，可有效解决超声点云补全难题，验证了引入测试时物理校正的有效性。

Abstract: Three-dimensional ultrasound (US) is a safe, radiation-free complementary modality to CT and X-rays for longitudinal monitoring, yet its segmentation-derived partial point clouds are extremely artifact-laden. Consequently, it is challenging to recover a clean and complete anatomical structure from such US point clouds. In this paper, we present UBone3D, a novel framework based on physics-rectified conditional flow matching (CFM) that performs point cloud completion directly from partial US observations. UBone3D models deterministic physics artifacts (e.g., surface thickening, streaking, dropouts) via a simulated physics proxy, and introduces test-time physics rectification to steer the shape completion. At inference, the completion is jointly steered by two decoupled forces: (1) anatomical plausibility enforced by a CT-trained generative shape prior, BoneFM, and (2) physics consistency enforced by USimNet in the ultrasound formation space. Extensive experiments on simulated and in-vivo data demonstrate significant improvements in reconstruction accuracy and anatomical fidelity over existing baselines.

</details>


### [54] [Harnessing Intrinsic Subject-Aware Attention for Controllable Multi-Subject Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11507)
*Niange Yu,Ye Tian,Biaolong Chen,Miao Lu,Aixi Zhang,Hao Jiang,Yunhai Tong,Pipei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DIAL框架，通过挖掘DiT内部注意力中固有的"内在空间定位图"(ISGM)信号，在低噪声阶段引导注意力实现可控保真度，在高噪声阶段零成本构建偏好对进行强化学习以缓解语义漂移，在OpenS2V-Eval上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多主体视频生成面临两大挑战：保真度强度不可控，以及潜在的语义漂移。作者希望通过分析DiT的内部机制来从根源上解决这两个问题。

Method: 通过分析DiT内部机制，发现部分注意力块天然形成能精确定位参考主体的"内在空间定位图"(ISGM)。基于此提出双阶段内在注意力利用框架DIAL：低噪声阶段用ISGM引导注意力机制，在无需重训练的情况下精确控制推理时的保真度强度；高噪声阶段用同一批图自动构建偏好对，以零额外成本进行强化学习。

Result: 在OpenS2V-Eval基准上，DIAL显著优于基线模型，持续提升身份一致性，并实现保真度强度的可控性。

Conclusion: DIAL表明DiT的内在注意力信号可同时服务于推理时的可控生成与训练时的强化学习对齐，为多主体视频生成的身份一致性与语义稳定性提供了统一且高效的解决思路。

Abstract: Multi-subject video generation faces two key challenges: uncontrollable fidelity strength and potential semantic drift. We address these by analyzing the internal mechanisms of Diffusion Transformers (DiTs). We found that certain attention blocks naturally form an Intrinsic Spatial Grounding Map (ISGM) that precisely locates reference subjects. Building on this insight, we propose Dual-phase Intrinsic Attention Leveraging (DIAL), a framework that uses these internal signals for both training and inference. In low-noise stages, we use ISGM to guide the attention mechanism, allowing precise control over fidelity strength during inference without retraining. In high-noise stages, we use these same maps to automatically build preference pairs at no additional cost for Reinforcement Learning (RL). This RL procedure effectively anchors the model's attention to reference subjects and mitigates semantic drift. Extensive experiments show that DIAL significantly outperforms baseline models on the OpenS2V-Eval benchmark, consistently improving identity consistency and enabling controllable fidelity strength.

</details>


### [55] [Prototype Matters: Modality-unified Prototype Self-distillation for Unsupervised Visible-infrared Person Re-identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11514)
*Menglin Wang,Xiaojin Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PoSeD框架，通过模态统一原型对比与原型引导自蒸馏，联合优化模态内与模态间相似性关系，提升无监督可见光-红外行人重识别的跨模态关联质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督可见光-红外行人重识别（VI-ReID）中，跨模态关联估计至关重要。现有最优传输方法存在硬标签分配过于刚性、未考虑聚类噪声的问题；同时仅施加跨模态对比也非最优，无法联合优化模态内与模态间的相似性关系。

Method: 提出新框架，充分利用原型进行跨模态学习：1）以模态统一的原型对比替代跨模态原型对比，同时优化模态内和跨模态相似性关系以增强模态不变性；2）将自原型作为稳定教师，通过原型引导的自蒸馏细化实例-原型在线关系；两部分在统一框架中联合优化，形成简洁有效的模型。

Result: 在标准VI-ReID基准上进行大量对比与分析实验，验证了所提方法的有效性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过模态统一原型对比与原型引导自蒸馏的统一优化，能有效缓解硬标签分配与聚类噪声问题，并联合优化模态内外相似性关系，从而提升无监督VI-ReID的跨模态关联性能。

Abstract: Estimating reliable cross-modality association is crucial to unsupervised visible-infrared person re-ID. While optimal transport is shown to be a practical solution for cross-modality association, it suffers from the rigidness of hard label assignment without considering the impact of cluster noise. Moreover, enforcing only cross-modality contrast is also suboptimal, as it fails to jointly optimize the similarity relation within and across modality. In this paper, we propose a novel framework for cross-modality learning by well exploitation of prototypes: First, instead of contrasting with cross-modality prototypes, we show that modality-unified prototypical contrast facilitates better modality invariance by jointly and simultaneously optimizing similarity relation within and across-modality. Taking self-prototype as a steady teacher, we further refine the instance-prototype online relation through prototype-guided self-distillation. The two components are optimized in a unified framework, leading to a simple yet effective model. On standard VI-ReID benchmarks, we perform extensive comparison and analysis, validating the effectiveness of our proposed method. Code is available at: https://github.com/Terminator8758/PoSeD.

</details>


### [56] [LoopVAE: Recurrent Depth Across Scales for Visual Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11516)
*Zhiying Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoopVAE 提出在视觉分词器中跨尺度共享参数的循环核心设计，用约 29M 参数在 ImageNet-256 上达到 0.28 rFID、32.54 dB PSNR，参数比 84M 参考 VAE 少约 65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层级式视觉分词器通常为不同空间尺度分配独立处理模块，导致参数量大。作者探究这种计算中有多少可以复用同一组参数。

Method: 提出 LoopVAE：在尺度内和跨尺度复用尺度与循环条件化的核心模块，仅分辨率变换的过渡部分保持独立；一个四块核心在每个编码器/解码器中执行 28 次块应用。并做了非对抗 Transformer 消融（相同执行图）与循环干预实验。

Result: ImageNet-256 上 29M 卷积模型达 0.28 rFID、32.54 dB PSNR（约 30 epoch 两阶段训练），参数比 84M 参考 VAE 少约 65%。Transformer 消融在全局共享下 PSNR/SSIM 具竞争力，但未共享块改善 LPIPS。循环干预显示完成训练好的循环可改善重建，小幅特征更新也会产生显著下游影响；截断暴露输出范围错误。运行时分析显示更少存储权重需要更多算术与更长运行时间。

Conclusion: LoopVAE 在卷积与 Transformer 算子、单/多分辨率潜变量接口下验证了跨尺度循环深度可作为视觉分词的一种参数共享设计轴。

Abstract: Hierarchical visual tokenizers typically allocate different processing blocks to different spatial scales. We ask how much of this computation can use the same parameters. LoopVAE reuses a scale- and loop-conditioned core within and across scales, while keeping resolution-changing transitions independent. A four-block core executes 28 block applications per encoder or decoder. On ImageNet-256, the 29M-parameter convolutional model reaches 0.28 rFID and 32.54 dB PSNR under an approximately 30-epoch two-stage training budget, using approximately 65% fewer parameters than the 84M reference VAEs. A non-adversarial Transformer ablation with the same execution graph finds competitive PSNR and SSIM under global sharing, although unshared blocks improve LPIPS. Targeted loop interventions show that completing the trained recurrence improves reconstruction and that even small feature updates can have substantial downstream effects. Truncation also exposes output-range errors, distinguishing useful recurrent computation from reliable early exit. Runtime profiling reveals the execution tradeoff: fewer stored weights require more arithmetic and longer runtime in the tested configurations. With convolutional and Transformer operators and single- or multi-resolution latent interfaces, LoopVAE establishes recurrent depth across scales as a parameter-sharing design axis for visual tokenization.

</details>


### [57] [Learning Interaction between Image and Layout Priors for Joint Image-Layout Generation in Design Templates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11519)
*Shirong Yang,Bo Yang,Ying Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出InterIL，一个将背景图像与前景布局在单一生成过程中联合生成的扩散模型，通过可学习的通信模块连接冻结的预训练图像与布局扩散骨干，显式建模双向图像-布局交互，并支持测试时引导以施加用户偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有平面设计生成工作多采用顺序范式，依次生成设计元素。作者认为顺序方案难以忠实刻画背景与布局之间的依赖关系（即联合的图像-布局分布），从而限制了生成设计模板的质量。

Method: 提出InterIL联合模型：在单一生成过程中同时生成背景图像与布局两种模态。用可学习的通信模块连接预训练图像扩散模型与布局扩散模型的骨干，显式建模双向图像-布局交互。训练时冻结图像与布局骨干以保留并利用大规模预训练的单模态先验，仅更新通信模块，使模型专注学习图像-布局交互。模型不含设计专用归纳偏置，以保留真实设计的原始特性；并引入测试时引导策略供用户施加偏好。

Result: 实验表明，与先前方法相比，InterIL在图像、布局以及图像-布局协调性方面均能生成显著更好的结果，输出更接近真实样本；并验证了在不重新训练的情况下于推理阶段施加用户偏好的灵活性。

Conclusion: 联合建模背景与布局的双向交互优于顺序生成范式，冻结单模态骨干、仅训练通信模块是一种有效且高效的策略，可在保持预训练先验的同时提升构图协调性，并支持可控推理。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the problem of graphic design template creation, which generates a background image and a layout of foreground elements over the background to form a harmonious composition from an input text.
  Prior work on graphic design generation mostly adopts a sequential paradigm, where design elements are generated sequentially. We argue that such a sequential scheme falls short of faithfully capturing the dependency between the background and layout (and thus the joint image-layout distribution), which limits the quality of generated design templates.
  To overcome this limitation, we propose a model, InterIL, which jointly generates the two modalities, background image and layout, in a single generative process. The novel design of our joint model connects the backbones of pretrained image and layout diffusion models with a learnable communication module to explicitly model bidirectional image-layout interaction. During training, the image and layout backbones are frozen to maintain and leverage the vast pretrained single-modality prior knowledge, while only the communication module is updated, so that the model can focus on learning image-layout interaction and thereby better capture the joint image-layout distribution for improved composition harmony.
  Our model has no design-specific inductive bias, which allows it to better preserve the original characteristics of realistic designs. We further introduce a test-time guidance strategy to enable users to impose their specific preferences on generated results.
  Our experiments show that, compared with prior approaches, our model can generate significantly better results in terms of image, layout and image-layout harmonization, producing outputs closer to real samples. We also demonstrate the flexibility of our model in enforcing user preferences at inference without retraining.

</details>


### [58] [World in World: Explore the World with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11548)
*Chenxi Song,Yanming Yang,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 World in World：一种无需训练、在推理阶段运行的接口，将异构控制证据转换为相机与时间标注的干净视觉状态，通过冻结因果视频模型的原生自注意力读取，实现相机控制重渲染、长时程回访与人体运动迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频世界模型支持交互式长时程探索，但灵活控制困难：新视角 rollout 需与原始事件同步、将观测内容置于目标视角、合理补全新暴露区域、并在回访时还原先前生成外观。现有方法依赖任务专用模块或额外训练，缺乏通用性。

Method: 训练无关的推理期接口。将多种控制证据（源视频观测、目标视角场景投影、引导新暴露主体区域补全的几何渲染、滚动缓存之外的已生成状态检索）转化为相机-时间标注的干净视觉状态，经冻结因果视频模型的原生自注意力读取。每个证据源带 token 级支持度与可用性调度；对应路由器结合持久点身份与几何建立 token 对应，将受支持查询导向匹配的源视频 token；证据级注意力 CFG（EWA）在同一次去噪前向中利用注意力响应，独立调节各辅助通道的增量贡献。同一接口支持相机控制重渲染、长时程回访与人体运动迁移。

Result: 在多样视角变化下的相机控制视频重渲染任务上评估，考察感知质量、时间一致性与相机跟随精度。

Conclusion: World in World 以统一、无需训练的推理期接口复用同一冻结主干，实现灵活可控的自回归视频世界模型探索。

Abstract: Autoregressive video world models enable interactive, long-horizon exploration, but flexible control remains challenging. Exploring a source video from new viewpoints requires the generated rollout to remain synchronised with the recorded event, place observed content in the requested view, plausibly complete newly exposed regions, and recover previously generated appearance on revisits. Existing methods typically address these requirements through task-specific modules or additional training. We present World in World, a training-free inference-time interface that converts heterogeneous control evidence into camera- and time-labelled clean visual states, which are read through the native self attention of a frozen causal video model. The evidence comprises source-video observations, target-view scene projections, geometry renderings that guide completion of newly exposed subject regions, and retrieved generated states beyond the rolling cache. Each evidence source carries token-level support and its own availability schedule. A correspondence router combines persistent point identities with geometry to establish token correspondences, guiding supported queries towards matching source-video tokens. Evidence-wise attention CFG (EWA) then independently regulates each auxiliary channel's additional contribution using attention responses from the same denoising forward pass. The shared interface supports camera-controlled rerendering, long-horizon revisiting, and human-motion transfer with the same frozen backbone. We evaluate World in World on camera-controlled video rerendering under diverse viewpoint changes, assessing perceptual quality, temporal consistency, and camera-following accuracy.

</details>


### [59] [A Comparative Evaluation of Pre-trained Convolutional Neural Networks for Melanoma Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11550)
*Wagner Moreno Schmitz,Marco Antonio de Castro Barbosa,Thiago Magalhães Amaral,Dalcimar Casanova,Jefferson Tales Oliva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究在同一训练协议下评估了ResNet50、VGG16、VGG19、MobileNet与InceptionV3在皮肤病变分类中的表现，覆盖皮肤镜与组织病理两类图像模态，结果显示模型性能随模态变化而变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑色素瘤早期诊断对提高患者生存率至关重要，但病变类型间视觉高度相似及图像采集条件差异使准确区分黑色素瘤与其他皮肤病变仍是临床难题。尽管已有大量预训练CNN架构，如何针对特定成像模态选择最合适的模型仍是未决问题。

Method: 在HAM10000、ISIC 2018和CR-AI4SkIN三个数据集上，采用统一训练协议对ResNet50、VGG16、VGG19、MobileNet和InceptionV3五种预训练CNN进行皮肤病变分类评估，分别针对皮肤镜图像与组织病理图像。

Result: 皮肤镜图像上准确率为71%（InceptionV3在ISIC 2018）至84%（ResNet50在HAM10000）；组织病理图像上准确率为72%（VGG19）至83%（ResNet50，CR-AI4SkIN数据集）。

Conclusion: 模型性能在皮肤镜与组织病理两种图像模态间存在差异，在皮肤镜图像上表现相近的架构在组织病理数据上表现不同，说明模型选择需考虑成像模态。

Abstract: Early diagnosis of melanoma is critical for improving patient survival rates. However, accurately distinguishing melanoma from other skin lesions remains a significant clinical challenge due to the high visual similarity among lesion types and variability in image acquisition conditions. Artificial intelligence, particularly machine learning, has emerged as a promising tool to support dermatological diagnosis by automating feature extraction from medical images. Among the available approaches, convolutional neural networks (CNNs) have demonstrated strong performance in image classification tasks, making them well-suited for analyzing both dermatoscopic and histopathological images, given their ability to capture hierarchical visual patterns relevant to lesion characterization. Nevertheless, despite numerous pre-trained CNN architectures having been proposed, selecting the most appropriate one for a given imaging modality remains an open challenge. In this study, we evaluate pre-trained convolutional neural networks (CNNs) for skin lesion classification using dermatoscopic and histopathological image datasets. Experiments were conducted on the HAM10000, ISIC 2018, and CR-AI4SkIN datasets, evaluating the ResNet50, VGG16, VGG19, MobileNet, and InceptionV3 architectures under the same training protocol. The experimental evaluation showed that the models achieved accuracies ranging from 71% (InceptionV3 on ISIC 2018) to 84% (ResNet50 on HAM10000) on dermatoscopic images. For histopathological images, accuracies ranged from 72% (VGG19) to 83% (ResNet50) on the CR-AI4SkIN dataset. The results demonstrate that model performance differs between dermatoscopic and histopathological image modalities, showing that architectures exhibiting similar performance on dermatoscopic images exhibit different performance on histopathological data.

</details>


### [60] [Learn the Solid, Not the File: Canonical Inputs for Neural Networks on CAD Boundary Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11573)
*Heinrich Jiang,Hager Yasser Mohamed,Alexander Hitt,Valeriia Lomakina,Henning Jiang,Jennifer Jang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: B-rep编码器对同一实体的不同B-rep表示不鲁棒；本文提出canonical region graph，具有重划分与刚体变换的理论不变性，在标准基准上匹敌最强基线且在各类扰动下稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一实体可由不同B-rep表示（不同操作、几何内核重建、导出设置对面片的重划分等），而现有B-rep编码器对这类表示变化不鲁棒，在标准基准扰动、CAD软件固有差异以及不同设计师建模同一零件时性能常严重崩溃。作者为此构建了FreeCAD人类数据集以验证该问题。

Method: 提出canonical region graph作为输入表示：其节点、特征与坐标系均从实体本身导出，而非依赖具体B-rep的划分，并在理论上给出重划分（repartitioning）与刚体运动（rigid motion）的不变性保证。

Result: 在标准基准上与最强基线表现相当；在所测试的每一类扰动下均保持稳定，而现有流行B-rep编码器往往性能急剧下降。

Conclusion: B-rep的表示歧义性是现有编码器的关键弱点；通过从实体本身导出规范化的区域图表示，可在不损失标准基准性能的前提下获得对重划分与刚体变换的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Boundary representation (B-rep) is the standard format used by modern CAD systems for parametric 3D models. It turns out, the exact same solid can be represented by different B-reps: for example, two engineers using different operations, a geometry kernel rebuilding the file, and an export setting repartitioning faces will lead to different B-reps even though the underlying solid remains the same.
  We show that existing B-rep encoders are not robust to variation in the B-rep with the same solid on perturbations applied to standard benchmarks, naturally occurring variations inherent to CAD software, and differences in how designers model the same part via a human dataset we created in FreeCAD. The performance of popular B-rep encoders often collapses catastrophically.
  We propose the canonical region graph, an input representation whose nodes, features and coordinate frame are derived from the solid itself and show theoretical invariance guarantees on repartitioning and rigid motions. It matches the strongest baseline on standard benchmarks, and is stable under every perturbation we test.

</details>


### [61] [OmniKVQuant: KV Cache Quantization for Omni-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11582)
*Suho Yoo,Hyunjong Ok,Jongmin Choi,Jihoo Jung,Joon Son Chung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出免训练的 OmniKVQuant 框架，解决多模态 KV cache 量化中的时间键漂移与异构值几何问题，在 Qwen2.5/3-Omni 上实现 2-bit KV cache 并保持七个音视频基准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全模态大模型同时接收音频、视频与文本，KV cache 显存开销显著增长；KV cache 量化在纯文本 LLM 中已成主流，但在全模态模型上尚未被探索。

Method: 分析代表性旋转式 KV 量化方法 TurboQuant 在多模态 cache 上的行为，发现时间键漂移与异构值几何两大问题；据此提出免训练框架 OmniKVQuant：(i) 在输入流的每个短时间窗口内设定键量化范围；(ii) 按模态分别旋转值。并提供融合 Triton 解码 kernel，在注意力中直接解包 2-bit cache。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-Omni 与 Qwen3-Omni 上，OmniKVQuant 支持 2-bit KV cache，并在七个音视频基准上大幅保持性能；融合 Triton kernel 避免构建稠密 FP16 cache。

Conclusion: OmniKVQuant 是首个面向全模态大模型的免训练 KV cache 量化方案，通过窗口化键范围与按模态值旋转有效缓解多模态量化难题，兼顾显存效率与性能，并已开源。

Abstract: As Omni-modal large language models (Omni-LLMs) take in audio, video and text together, their KV cache memory cost grows. KV cache quantization is the de facto approach in text-only LLMs, but its application to Omni-LLMs remains unexplored. In this paper, we analyze how TurboQuant, a representative rotation-based KV cache quantization method, behaves on multimodal caches and identify two critical issues: temporal key drift and heterogeneous value geometry. To address these, we propose OmniKVQuant, a training-free framework that (i) sets the key quantization range over each short window of the input stream; and (ii) rotates values separately per modality. On Qwen2.5-Omni and Qwen3-Omni, OmniKVQuant enables 2-bit KV caches while substantially preserving performance across seven audio-visual benchmarks. We further provide a fused Triton decode kernel that unpacks the 2-bit cache during attention, so no dense FP16 cache is ever built. Code: https://github.com/kaistmm/OmniKVQuant

</details>


### [62] [MMGait: Benchmarking and Unifying Gait Recognition across Heterogeneous Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11601)
*Saihui Hou,Chenye Wang,Qingyuan Cai,Aoqi Li,Yongzhen Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多传感器步态基准 MMGait 与统一模型 OmniGait++，将单模态、跨模态和多模态步态识别统一到共享身份空间，验证异构传感下统一识别的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有步态识别多依赖 RGB 视频或其衍生的轮廓与姿态，无法系统考察人类行走产生的异构光度、几何与运动线索；且单模态、模态对与融合配置通常由分别训练的专家模型处理，存在可扩展性问题。

Method: 构建 MMGait：大规模多传感器基准，将可见光、红外、深度、LiDAR 与雷达观测在序列级对应，覆盖外观、轮廓、几何、运动与身体结构等模态，并采用统一的冒充者增强协议。评估单模态识别、基于有向检索的跨模态识别以及使用任务特定专家的多模态识别。提出 Omni-Modal Gait Recognition 任务，OmniGait++ 采用模态特定前端加共享身份编码器，保留模态相关线索并学习可比较的身份描述；锚点引导融合模块聚合不同规模模态子集，无需帧级同步；单一联合训练权重覆盖三类设置。

Result: 模态排名随探测条件变化，跨模态对齐仍较困难，融合常带来互补增益。OmniGait++ 在许多共享设置下与任务特定专家相当，并可扩展到固定配对模型无法支持的高基数融合。

Conclusion: MMGait 可作为异构步态感知的通用测试平台，证明了在模态可用性变化条件下实现统一识别的可行性。

Abstract: Gait recognition is commonly studied using RGB videos or their derived silhouettes and poses. Yet human walking produces heterogeneous photometric, geometric, and motion cues that cannot be systematically examined with RGB-centered benchmarks. We present MMGait, a large-scale multi-sensor benchmark that brings visible, infrared, depth, LiDAR, and radar observations into sequence-level correspondence. It provides diverse modalities spanning appearance, contours, geometry, motion, and body structure. Under a shared impostor-augmented protocol, we evaluate single-modal recognition, cross-modal recognition via directed retrieval, and multi-modal recognition using task-specific experts. Across settings, modality rankings vary with probe conditions, cross-modal alignment remains difficult, and fusion often provides complementary gains. This analysis exposes a scalability problem: individual modalities, modality pairs, and fusion configurations are typically handled by separately trained experts. We formulate Omni-Modal Gait Recognition, which unifies single-modal, cross-modal, and multi-modal recognition within a shared identity space. OmniGait++ uses modality-specific front ends followed by a shared identity encoder to preserve modality-dependent cues while learning comparable identity descriptors. An anchor-guided fusion module aggregates modality subsets of varying size without frame-level synchronization. A jointly trained checkpoint covers all three recognition settings and accommodates modality subsets of different compositions and cardinalities. Experiments show OmniGait++ remains competitive with task-specific experts in many shared settings and extends to higher-cardinality fusion unavailable to fixed-pair models. The results establish MMGait as a common testbed for heterogeneous gait sensing and demonstrate the feasibility of unified recognition under varying modality availability.

</details>


### [63] [LangStreet: Persistent Language Fields for Anchor-Decoded Street Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11616)
*Runyi Yang,Deheng Zhang,Xiaoye Wang,Mengjiao Ma,Lei Sun,Kanzhi Wu,Ajad Chhatkuli,Luc Van Gool,Danda Pani Paudel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"语义所有权"思想构建持久语言场，用锚点+低秩槽残差表示，实现视图条件高斯泼溅场景下的高效准确语言场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言高斯场隐含假设"承载语义的基元跨视图可识别"，但在可扩展的锚点解码表示中，持久锚点生成随相机变化的子高斯，其几何与外观随视图改变，使该假设失效，需重新设计持久语义表示机制。

Method: 核心思想为语义所有权：瞬态子高斯仅路由观测，持久解码槽及其父锚点拥有语言场。利用alpha合成责任在槽上累积加性方向证据，其统计量可精确边缘化到锚点；用锚点对齐证据补全弱支持槽并保持锚点方向，通过锚点相对语义坐标下的低秩残差表示槽细节。提出三种变体：Ours(base)存锚点特征+紧凑槽残差，Ours(light)仅存锚点特征，Ours(max)显式存全维补全槽特征。

Result: 无需场景特定语义优化，Ours(base)在KITTI、Virtual KITTI、Waymo上接近Ours(max)。KITTI上Ours(base)达34.19 2D mIoU，有效特征占用2.72 GiB；Ours(max)为34.20 mIoU、12.90 GiB。精度-存储趋势在两个数据集上一致。

Conclusion: 视图条件splat上的语言场需要持久语义所有权、守恒证据，以及平衡稳定性、细节与表示成本的层级结构；该框架在保持精度的同时显著压缩存储。

Abstract: Language Gaussian fields implicitly assume that the primitive carrying semantics remains identifiable across views. This assumption breaks in scalable anchor-decoded representations, where persistent anchors generate view-conditioned child Gaussians whose geometry and appearance vary with the camera. We introduce Ours, a persistent language field for such structured Gaussian scenes. Our key idea is semantic ownership: transient children route observations, while persistent decoder slots and their parent anchors own the language field. We use alpha-compositing responsibilities to accumulate additive directional evidence at slots; these statistics marginalize exactly to anchors. We then complete weakly supported slots with anchor-aligned evidence while preserving the anchor direction, and represent slot detail through low-rank residuals in anchor-relative semantic coordinates. Our primary model, Ours (base), stores anchor features together with compact slot residuals. Ours (light) retains only anchor features, whereas Ours (max) stores the full-dimensional completed slot features explicitly. Without scene-specific semantic optimization, Ours (base) nearly matches Ours (max) across KITTI, Virtual KITTI, and Waymo. On KITTI, it achieves 34.19 2D mIoU with a 2.72 GiB effective feature footprint, compared with 34.20 mIoU and 12.90 GiB for Ours (max). The same accuracy-storage trend holds on Virtual KITTI and Waymo. These results show that language fields on view-conditioned splats require persistent semantic ownership, conserved evidence, and a hierarchy that balances stability, detail, and representation cost. Our code, checkpoints, and benchmark suite will be publicly available.

</details>


### [64] [Vidu S2: Real-Time Interactive, Editable, and Spatial Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11638)
*Jintao Zhang,Kai Jiang,Jintao Chen,Xu Wang,Deyuan Liu,Jungang Li,Dechuang Chen,Ming Lin,Jingjiang Zhou,Haopeng Jin,Qi Jia,Xiaohang Wang,Yaole Wang,Zhanqiang Zhang,Ran Li,Zhengkun Huang,Shuyue Xiong,Yuji Wang,Zikun Dai,Hui He,Yang Luo,Mang Ning,Weiqi Feng,Chengyang Ye,Xinyue Lin,Min Zhao,Hongzhou Zhu,Hengkai Tan,Zeyuan Wang,Chendong Xiang,Kaiwen Zheng,Zhijie Deng,Fan Bao,Jianfei Chen,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Vidu S2 系统，包含实时交互数字人模型 Vidu S2-Avatar 与实时视频编辑模型 Vidu S2-Editing，并探索了二者的实时空间视频生成可行性。实验表明 Vidu S2 优于所有基线方法，并提供在线演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相较于前代 Vidu S1，需要在实时性、分辨率、动态参考与指令遵循能力上进一步提升交互式数字人与视频编辑的性能，并探索实时空间视频生成的可行性。

Method: 构建 Vidu S2 系统：Vidu S2-Avatar 支持实时 720p 视频生成、可随时更新的动态参考以及更强的指令遵循（如舞蹈）；Vidu S2-Editing 支持对视频流进行实时编辑，涵盖风格渲染、服装替换、角色替换与背景替换；同时探索两者的实时空间视频生成。

Result: 实验显示 Vidu S2 在所有基线上均取得更优表现，并提供了可交互的在线演示（https://vidu.com/vidu-stream）。

Conclusion: Vidu S2 在实时交互数字人生成与实时视频编辑任务上实现了性能领先，并初步验证了实时空间视频生成的可行性，为实时视频生成应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present Vidu S2, which comprises Vidu S2-Avatar, a real-time interactive digital-character model, and Vidu S2-Editing, a real-time video editing model. Moreover, we explore the feasibility of real-time spatial video generation for both Vidu S2-Avatar and Vidu S2-Editing. Compared with Vidu S1, Vidu S2-Avatar supports real-time 720p video generation, generation with dynamic references that can be updated at any moment, and stronger instruction following, such as dancing. Vidu S2-Editing supports editing a video stream in real time, including style rendering, clothing replacement, character replacement, and background replacement. Experiments show that Vidu S2 outperforms all baselines. A playable online demo is available at https://vidu.com/vidu-stream.

</details>


### [65] [Self-Supervised Cardiac Phase Detection via Single-Parameter Latent Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11650)
*John Bonnici,Matthew Baugh,Aleksandra Kulbaka,Sarah Cechnicka,Bernhard Kainz,Alberto Gomez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将"心动周期是一维信号"的生理先验转化为单参数隐轨道约束，实现可解释的心相位建模，在EchoNet-Dynamic上无标注训练后显著提升ED定位并匹敌ES定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声心动图中ED/ES关键帧的人工选取主观性强、存在显著的观察者间差异。现有自监督方法分为两类：一是规定严格周期性轨迹，可解释但假设过强；二是从重建或配准目标学习无约束低维运动子空间，但心相位隐含在表示中，ED/ES需依赖后处理几何方法从学到的轨迹中恢复。因此需要兼顾可解释性与对不规则心跳灵活性的表示。

Method: 将心相位作为一维信号的生理观察转化为先验：把潜运动分量约束为单参数隐轨道，即潜空间中的全局线性轨迹，由有界标量相位变量索引。再将该变量通过正弦非线性映射，得到具有一致时间顺序的振荡运动信号，可直接从学到的相位信号识别ED与ES。该归纳偏置在保持对不规则心律灵活性的同时，提供可解释的心周期表示。在EchoNet-Dynamic上无标注训练。

Result: 最小化的单参数心相位模型学到了有效的隐轨道，在ED定位上显著优于先前最先进方法，在ES定位上与之持平，同时使用更受约束的表示和更少的训练轮次。

Conclusion: 有原则的生理归纳偏置能够匹配甚至超越更复杂表示的性能，说明一维相位先验是建模心相位的有效且高效的方式。

Abstract: Accurate identification of end-diastole (ED) and end-systole (ES) in echocardiography underpins the quantification of ventricular function, yet manual selection of these key frames is subjective and introduces clinically significant inter-operator variability. Recent self-supervised methods either prescribe strict periodic trajectories or learn an unconstrained low-dimensional motion subspace from reconstruction or registration objectives. The former offers interpretability but imposes restrictive assumptions on temporal progression, whereas the latter leaves cardiac phase implicit and ED/ES must be recovered through post-hoc geometric processing of the learned trajectory. We translate the physiological observation that cardiac phase is a one-dimensional signal into a prior by constraining the latent motion component to a single-parameter latent orbit, i.e., a global linear trajectory in latent space indexed by a bounded scalar phase variable. Mapping this variable through a sinusoidal nonlinearity yields an oscillatory motion signal with consistent temporal ordering, enabling direct identification of ED and ES from the learned phase signal. This inductive bias allows the model to capture an interpretable representation of the cardiac cycle, while maintaining flexibility to capture irregular heartbeats. Trained on EchoNet-Dynamic without annotations, our minimal single-parameter cardiac phase model learns an effective latent orbit, significantly improves upon the previous state of the art in ED localisation and matches it in ES localisation while using a more constrained representation and fewer training epochs. This demonstrates that a principled physiological inductive bias can match or exceed the performance of more complex representations. Code is available at: https://github.com/BonniciJ/OrbitalEcho/

</details>


### [66] [Multimodal Taxonomic Conditioning for Generative Plankton Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11673)
*Daniela Ivanova,Ozgu Goksu,Nicolas Pugeault*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于分类学条件的浮游生物合成图像生成方法：用排序对比目标适配CLIP编码器处理深层、不规则分类体系，冻结后以参数高效扩散Transformer生成合成图像，并从分布保真度与下游分类器效用两方面评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化浮游生物成像产生的数据集呈严重长尾分布，生态上最重要的稀有类群图像过少，难以可靠地训练或评估分类器。因此需要为稀有类群合成高质量、符合分类学语义的图像以缓解数据稀缺。

Method: 1) 在大规模浮游生物语料上适配一个CLIP编码器，将对比目标扩展为排序对比目标，以支持深层且不规则（ragged）的分类体系；2) 冻结该编码器，用以条件化一个参数高效（parameter-efficient）的扩散Transformer来生成合成浮游生物图像；3) 在分布保真度与下游分类器效用两个维度上评估合成样本质量。

Result: 摘要未给出具体数值结果，仅说明从分布保真度（distributional fidelity）和下游分类器效用（downstream classifier utility）两方面对合成样本质量进行了评估，以验证生成数据的有效性与可用性。

Conclusion: 分类学条件化的合成图像生成可作为缓解浮游生物长尾数据稀缺、支撑稀有类群分类器训练与评估的可行途径，其价值需同时由分布层面的保真度和任务层面的分类器效用共同衡量。

Abstract: Automated plankton imaging produces severely long-tailed datasets, where the rare taxa of greatest ecological interest have too few images to train or evaluate classifiers reliably. We generate synthetic plankton imagery conditioned on taxonomy: a CLIP encoder is adapted on a large plankton corpus with a ranked contrastive objective extended to deep, ragged taxonomies, then frozen to condition a parameter-efficient diffusion transformer. We evaluate synthetic sample quality on distributional fidelity and downstream classifier utility.

</details>


### [67] [Single-Stream Multi-Feature Fusion with Temporal Robustness for Gait Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11680)
*Shirong Lyu,Silu Quan,Yixuan Ding,Chengpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出SV-GCN——一种具有时间不变性的单流多特征融合框架，用于3D骨架步态情感识别，在E-Gait数据集上取得与SOTA相当的性能，并对序列长度与帧率变化展现强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D骨架步态情感识别面临标注成本高、数据稀缺、异构数据泛化差等问题；此外序列长度可变、帧率不一致导致模型对时间尺度敏感，传统多流结构则带来复杂度。

Method: 提出SV-GCN单流多特征融合框架：引入帧内相对运动特征以消除帧率敏感性，并在浅层嵌入异构线索实现早期融合，避免多流复杂度；针对变长序列设计全局掩码引导的有效帧时空图卷积模块，首次在该领域引入帧率不敏感性。

Result: 在E-Gait数据集上，性能与当前最先进方法相当，并在不同序列长度与帧率下表现出强泛化性。

Conclusion: SV-GCN为在大规模骨架动作识别数据集上进行预训练提供了可行路径，缓解了标注成本高与数据稀缺的制约。

Abstract: 3D skeleton-based gait emotion recognition faces high annotation costs, data scarcity, and poor generalization on heterogeneous data. This paper proposes SV-GCN, a single-stream multi-feature fusion framework with temporal invariance. We introduce intra-frame relative motion features to eliminate frame-rate sensitivity and embed heterogeneous cues at shallow layers, enabling early fusion without multi-stream complexity. For variable-length sequences, we design a global mask-guided valid-frame spatio-temporal graph convolution module, introducing frame-rate insensitivity for the first time in this domain. On the E-Gait dataset, our method achieves performance comparable to state-of-the-art while demonstrating strong generalization across varying sequence lengths and frame rates, offering a viable pathway for pre-training on large-scale skeleton-based action recognition datasets.

</details>


### [68] [Spectral Adapters for Segment Anything Model-based Segmentation of Colorectal Liver Metastases in Computed Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11703)
*Ramtin Mojtahedi,Mohammad Hamghalam,Jacob J. Peoples,Natalie Gangai,Mithat Gonen,Yun Shin Chun,HyunSeon Christine Kang,Richard K. G. Do,Amber L. Simpson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两种参数高效的光谱适配器DiSECT与SiGA，用于将SAM适配至结直肠癌肝转移CT分割，SiGA在单点提示下达0.77 Dice，无提示下达0.76 Dice，接近nnU-Net基线，且DiSECT仅需0.14M可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结直肠癌肝转移（CRLM）在增强CT中的精确分割对疗效评估、手术规划与随访至关重要。然而将SAM等大模型高效适配至该医学分割任务时，面临可训练参数多、计算开销大的问题，因此需要参数高效的适配方法。

Method: 提出两种参数高效光谱适配器：DiSECT通过对冻结权重进行奇异值分解，将残差更新约束到主导光谱方向；SiGA在其基础上通过多层感知机引入全局与输入条件门控。在446例增强CT体数据（355训练/91测试）上评估，与LoRA、QLoRA、卷积适配器（CAD）及3D nnU-Net基线对比，考察单点、三点、边界框和无提示四种提示模式。

Result: SiGA在单点提示下取得最佳性能：Dice 0.77、IoU 0.69、HD95 35.39 mm；无提示推理下达到Dice 0.76、IoU 0.68、HD95 46.76 mm，与nnU-Net基线（Dice 0.758）相当。DiSECT仅使用0.14M可训练参数。

Conclusion: 光谱适配器能够以极少的可训练参数高效地将SAM适配于CRLM分割，在保持较强精度的同时显著降低参数开销，验证了光谱方向约束与实例引导门控在医学图像分割中的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of colorectal liver metastases (CRLM) in contrast-enhanced computed tomography (CT) is important for response assessment, surgical planning, and follow-up. We propose two parameter-efficient spectral adapters for the Segment Anything Model (SAM): the Directional Spectral Adapter (DiSECT) and Spectral Instance-Guided Adapter (SiGA). DiSECT uses singular value decomposition of frozen weights to constrain residual updates to leading spectral directions, while SiGA adds global and input-conditioned gating through a multilayer perceptron. We evaluate these methods on 446 contrast-enhanced CT volumes (355 training, 91 testing) and compare them with LoRA, QLoRA, convolutional adapters (CAD), and a 3D nnU-Net baseline. Experiments consider single-point, three-point, bounding-box, and no-prompt regimes. SiGA achieves the best single-point performance with a Dice score of 0.77, IoU of 0.69, and HD95 of 35.39 mm. Under no-prompt inference, SiGA reaches 0.76 Dice, 0.68 IoU, and 46.76 mm HD95, comparable to the nnU-Net baseline (0.758 Dice). DiSECT uses only 0.14 million trainable parameters. These results show that spectral adapters can efficiently adapt SAM for CRLM segmentation while retaining strong accuracy with limited trainable parameters.

</details>


### [69] [Language-Augmented Semantic Priors for B-Spline Surface Fitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11708)
*Yunzhong Lou,Yusheng Luo,Jiahao Li,Yu Song,Xiangdong Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LASP是一个语言增强语义先验框架，利用大语言模型从程序化建模历史中推理出结构化、求解器可用的B样条先验，作为现有几何求解器之上的语义推理层，引导B样条拟合更符合设计意图并提升语义一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CAD几何内核在进行曲面拟合与参数化时仍严重依赖预设的启发式初始化，且建模历史中蕴含的过程语义与设计意图在几何生成阶段基本被忽略。这种脱节造成高层设计意图与求解器可执行的几何配置之间存在鸿沟，导致拟合结果次优且语义不一致。

Method: LASP不修改几何内核本身，而是在现有求解器之上构建语义推理层。它首先将建模历史转译为包含设计意图、几何上下文与功能关系的丰富文本描述，随后用微调后的LLM预测结构化的B样条先验参数。训练采用两阶段方案，融合局部几何规律性与长程上下文依赖，从而得到可解释且语义连贯的先验。

Result: 实验表明，与传统机器学习方案相比，语言驱动的推理能够作为几何求解的强大归纳偏置，为常规B样条拟合过程提供归纳信号，使其更准确地体现预期设计目标并展现更高的语义连贯性。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了语言引导几何优化的新范式，说明语言驱动推理可有效弥合设计意图与几何求解之间的语义鸿沟，为现代CAD系统中的几何优化提供了新的思路。

Abstract: The use of B-splines and Non-Uniform Rational B-Splines surfaces constitutes the mathematical foundation of contemporary computer-aided design (CAD) systems. Despite long-term progress, geometric kernels in traditional CAD still rely heavily on predetermined heuristic initialization for surface fitting and parameterization. Meanwhile, the procedural semantics and design intent encoded in modeling histories are largely ignored during geometry generation. This disconnect creates a gap between high-level design intent and solver-executable geometric configuration, often leading to suboptimal and semantically inconsistent fitting results. To bridge this gap, we introduce LASP, a Language-Augmented Semantic Priors framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) to infer structured, solver-usable B-spline priors from procedural modeling histories. Rather than modifying the geometric kernel itself, LASP operates as a semantic reasoning layer above existing solvers. It first translates modeling histories into rich textual descriptions that capture design intent, geometric context, and functional relationships, and then uses a fine-tuned LLM to predict structured B-spline prior parameters. LASP is trained through a two-stage scheme that combines local geometric regularities with long-range contextual dependencies, producing priors that are both interpretable and semantically coherent. This approach furnishes inductive signals that direct the conventional B-spline fitting process toward solutions that more accurately encapsulate the intended design objectives and demonstrate heightened semantic coherence. Compared to traditional machine learning schemes, the experiments demonstrate that language-driven reasoning can serve as a powerful inductive bias for geometric solving, establishing a new paradigm of language-guided geometric optimization in modern CAD systems.

</details>


### [70] [Revisiting Avatar-As-Image: High-Fidelity Registration is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11722)
*Margaret Kostyrko,Yuxuan Xue,Garvita Tiwari,Gerard Pons-Moll*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 AvaImg，一种多阶段优化流水线，用于从任意穿衣服装扫描中生成高保真 SMPL(-X)+D 配准与 UV 纹理映射，并通过与图像基础模型的兼容性验证其表示的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将3D穿衣人体表示为标准2D UV纹理与位移图，便于用预训练图像网络处理、生成和编辑3D化身；但该表示依赖高保真配准（扫描对齐与对应），此前该前提从未满足，导致基于UV的方法质量受限。目前也缺少能从任意穿衣服装扫描中生成高保真SMPL(-X)+D配准与UV纹理的公开方法。

Method: AvaImg采用多阶段优化流水线：通过签名环绕数（signed winding numbers）强制“身体在衣物内”约束，并利用三级效率级联使该方法可行（运行时间约减少10倍，存储节省约95%）；使用从粗到细的位移优化恢复精细表面细节。

Result: 在六个数据集上，AvaImg在人体拟合、形状估计和表面配准方面优于所有基线；生成带纹理的配准结果与扫描近乎难以区分（PSNR=34.48dB）。将UV图通过冻结的FLUX VAE自编码，仅增加0.76mm Chamfer误差，且生成的图位于自然图像分布内。

Conclusion: AvaImg填补了从任意穿衣服装扫描生成高保真SMPL(-X)+D配准与UV纹理的空白，并验证其“Avatar-as-Image”表示与图像基础模型高度兼容，支持利用2D生成先验进行3D化身生成。代码、数据和Singularity容器将公开。

Abstract: The representation of 3D clothed humans as standardized 2D UV texture and displacement maps over an underlying body model has long been studied. This compact representation is enticing as it enables pretrained image networks to process, generate, and edit 3D avatars, but is only useful if scans are accurately aligned and brought into correspondence via high-fidelity registration. This prerequisite has never been met, which we argue explains the limited quality of prior UV-based methods for clothed humans. Despite its significance, no public method produces high-fidelity SMPL(-X)+D registrations with UV texture from arbitrary clothed scans. We present AvaImg, a multi-stage optimization pipeline, to close this gap: it enforces body-inside-clothing constraint via signed winding numbers, made viable by a three-level efficiency cascade (~10x runtime reduced, ~95% storage saved), and recovers fine surface detail using coarse-to-fine displacement optimization. AvaImg outperforms all baselines in body fitting, shape estimation, and surface registration across six datasets, yielding textured registrations near-indistinguishable from scans (PSNR=34.48dB). For validation of AvaImg's Avatar-as-Image representation as imminently compatible with image foundation models, we auto-encode our UV maps via the frozen FLUX VAE. This achieves only 0.76mm added Chamfer error relative to scan and shows that the resulting maps lie within natural-image distributions, supporting the use of 2D generative priors for 3D avatar generation. Code, data, and Singularity containers will be at https://yuxuan-xue.com/avaimg.

</details>


### [71] [Logit Refiner: Improving Visual Autoregressive Models via Intra-Scale Dependency Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11804)
*Meimingwei Li,Stefan Andreas Baumann,Felix Krause,Björn Ommer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出视觉自回归模型（VAR）在每一尺度内并行预测所有token，等价于均值场近似，丢弃了同尺度token间的空间依赖，导致局部不连贯；提出轻量级Logit Refiner模块，以自回归方式顺序采样token恢复尺度内依赖，仅增加约10%参数和<5%训练算力，可即插即用，显著提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
